If we use Sinon to wait for that\r\n  // timeout to trigger, then that\'ll play back one move... but it will\r\n  // still leave various promises unresolved. `handleMove` won\'t return,\r\n  // which means `handleRedo` won\'t return, which means the next replay\r\n  // timeout won\'t be created. This means we can\'t just wait for any\r\n  // amount of time... we need create another promise which will resolve\r\n  // along with those other unresolved promises...\r\n  const advanceReplaySteps = async (n: number) => {\r\n    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\r\n      const promise = new Promise(resolve => {\r\n        setTimeout(resolve, replayTimeout)\r\n      })\r\n\r\n      // ...then `await` THAT\r\n      await promise\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  it(\'lets you replay a too-long replay\', async () => {\r\n    const warn = sinon.stub(console, \'warn\')\r\n\r\n    const game = AAAA AAAA\')\r\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\r\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\r\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\r\n    ])\r\n    prompt.restore()\r\n\r\n    // Play beyond the end of the supplied replay.\r\n    await advanceReplaySteps(300)\r\n\r\n    assert.deepEqual(warn.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\r\n      [\'Ignoring input replay step because mode is\', \'GAME_OVER\']\r\n    ])\r\n\r\n    game.unmount()\r\n    warn.restore()\r\n  })\r\n\r\n  it(\'lets you carry on playing after an incomplete replay Game halted.\')\r\n    game.unmount()\r\n  })\r\n\r\n  it(\'lets you decide not to replay anything\', async () => {\r\n    const game = renderGame()\r\n\r\n    const prompt = sinon.stub(window, \'prompt\')\r\n    prompt.returns(null)\r\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\r\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\r\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\r\n    ])\r\n    prompt.restore()\r\n    game.unmount()\r\n  })\r\n\r\n  // The act of initiating a replay causes a timeout to be created which\r\n  // will play one step of the replay. If we use Sinon to wait for that\r\n  // timeout to trigger, then that\'ll play back one move... but it will\r\n  // still leave various promises unresolved. `handleMove` won\'t return,\r\n  // which means `handleRedo` won\'t return, which means the next replay\r\n  // timeout won\'t be created. This means we can\'t just wait for any\r\n  // amount of time... we need create another promise which will resolve\r\n  // along with those other unresolved promises...\r\n  const advanceReplaySteps = async (n: number) => {\r\n    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\r\n      const promise = new Promise(resolve => {\r\n        setTimeout(resolve, replayTimeout)\r\n      })\r\n\r\n      // ...then `await` THAT\r\n      await promise\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  it(\'lets you replay a too-long replay\', async () => {\r\n    const warn = sinon.stub(console, \'warn\')\r\n\r\n    const game = renderGame()\r\n\r\n    // Replay keeps pressing Down after game over\r\n    const prompt = sinon.stub(window, \'prompt\')\r\n    prompt.returns(\'AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA\')\r\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\r\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\r\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\r\n    ])\r\n    prompt.restore()\r\n\r\n    // Play beyond the end of the supplied replay.\r\n    await advanceReplaySteps(300)\r\n\r\n    assert.deepEqual(warn.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\r\n      [\'Ignoring input replay step because mode is\', \'GAME_OVER\']\r\n    ])\r\n\r\n    game.unmount()\r\n    warn.restore()\r\n  })\r\n\r\n  it(\'lets you carry on playing after an incomplete replay ends\', async () => {\r\n    const game = renderGame()\r\n\r\n    // Replay is an incomplete game: a single "down" move\r\n    const prompt = sinon.stub(window, \'prompt\')\r\n    prompt.returns(\'D\')\r\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\r\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\r\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\r\n    ])\r\n    prompt.restore()\r\n\r\n    // Play the single replay step\r\n    await advanceReplaySteps(2)\r\n\r\n    // Not stuck in replay mode; the user can carry on playing\r\n    assert.equal(screen.queryAllByTestId(\'bottom\').length, 0)\r\n    assert.equal(screen.queryAllByTestId(\'down\').length, 1)\r\n\r\n    // And the user can actually continue playing\r\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'down\'))\r\n    assert.equal(screen.queryAllByTestId(/well__cell--live/).length, 4)\r\n\r\n    game.unmount()\r\n  })\r\n\r\n  it(\'lets you undo and stops replaying when a replay is in progress\', async () => {\r\n    const game = renderGame()\r\n\r\n    // Replay is an incomplete game\r\n    const prompt = sinon.stub(window, \'prompt\')\r\n    prompt.returns(\'AAAA AAAA AAAA\')\r\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\r\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\r\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\r\n    ])\r\n    prompt.restore()\r\n\r\n    // Play beyond the end of the supplied replay\r\n    await advanceReplaySteps(60)\r\n\r\n    await user.keyboard(\'{Control>}z{/Control}\')\r\n    // TODO: assert that `wellStateId` is now decremented?\r\n    game.unmount()\r\n  })\r\n})\r\n'}, 'isError': False, 'timestamp': 1787269698079}

async (n: number) => {\r\n    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\r\n      const promise = new Promise(resolve => {\r\n        setTimeout(resolve, replayTimeout)\r\n      })\r\n\r\n      // ...then `await` THAT\r\n      await promise\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  it(\'lets you replay a too-long replay\', async () => {\r\n    const warn = sinon.stub(console, \'warn\')\r\n\r\n    const game = AAAA AAAA\')\r\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\r\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\r\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\r\n    ])\r\n    prompt.restore()\r\n\r\n    // Play beyond the end of the supplied replay.\r\n    await advanceReplaySteps(300)\r\n\r\n    assert.deepEqual(warn.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\r\n If we use Sinon to wait for that\r\n  // timeout to trigger, then that\'ll play back one move... but it will\r\n  // still leave various promises unresolved. `handleMove` won\'t return,\r\n  // which means `handleRedo` won\'t return, which means the next replay\r\n  // timeout won\'t be created. This means we can\'t just wait for any\r\n  // amount of time... we need create another promise which will resolve\r\n  // along with those other unresolved promises...\r\n  const advanceReplaySteps = async (n: number) => {\r\n    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\r\n      const promise = new Promise(resolve => {\r\n        setTimeout(resolve, replayTimeout)\r\n      })\r\n\r\n      // ...then `await` THAT\r\n      await promise\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  it(\'lets you replay a too-long replay\', async () => {\r\n    const warn = sinon.stub(console, \'warn\')\r\n\r\n    const game = renderGame()\r\n\r\n    // Replay keeps pressing Down after game over\r\n    const prompt = sinon.stub(window, \'prompt\')\r\n    prompt.returns(\'AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA\')\r\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\r\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\r\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\r\n    ])\r\n    prompt.restore()\r\n\r\n    // Play beyond the end of the supplied replay.\r\n    await advanceReplaySteps(300)\r\n\r\n    assert.deepEqual(warn.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\r\n      [\'Ignoring input replay step because mode is\', \'GAME_OVER\']\r\n    ])\r\n\r\n    game.unmount()\r\n    warn.restore()\r\n  })\r\n\r\n  it(\'lets you carry on playing from the same state after an incomplete replay ends\', async () => {\r\n    // Snapshot the rendered well so we can compare two games cell by cell\r\n    const getWell = (game: ReturnType<typeof render>) =>\r\n      Array.from(game.container.querySelectorAll(\'table tr\'), row =>\r\n        Array.from(row.querySelectorAll(\'td\'), td => td.getAttribute(\'class\'))\r\n      )\r\n\r\n    const game = renderGame()\r\n\r\n    // Replay is an incomplete game: a single "down" move\r\n    const prompt = sinon.stub(window, \'prompt\')\r\n    prompt.returns(\'D\')\r\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\r\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\r\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\r\n    ])\r\n    prompt.restore()\r\n\r\n    // Play the single replay step\r\n    await advanceReplaySteps(2)\r\n\r\n    // Not stuck in replay mode; The same three\r\n    // "down" moves (one from the replay, two by hand) must reach the exact\r\n    // same well, proving the replay handed over its state rather than restarting.\r\n    const manualGame = renderGame()\r\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'start-button\'))\r\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'down\'))\r\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'down\'))\r\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'down\'))\r\n    const manualWell = getWell(manualGame)\r\n\r\n    assert.deepEqual(continuedWell, manualWell)\r\n    manualGame.unmount()\r\n  })\r\n\r\n  it(\'lets you undo and stops replaying when a replay is in progress\', async () => {\r\n    const game = renderGame()\r\n\r\n    // Replay is an incomplete game\r\n    const prompt = sinon.stub(window, \'prompt\')\r\n    prompt.returns(\'AAAA AAAA AAAA\')\r\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\r\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\r\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\r\n    ])\r\n    prompt.restore()\r\n\r\n    // Play beyond the end of the supplied replay\r\n    await advanceReplaySteps(60)\r\n\r\n    await user.keyboard(\'{Control>}z{/Control}\')\r\n    // TODO: assert that `wellStateId` is now decremented?\r\n    game.unmount()\r\n  })\r\n})\r\n'}, 'isError': False, 'timestamp': 1787270085172}
